##FUNCTION

na_0 <-function(dataset, col){
  dataset[, col] <- ifelse(is.na(dataset[, col]), 0, dataset[, col])
  return(dataset)
}

#data1(77554명) baseline 정리#

                          ifelse(data1$AGE >=60 &data1$AGE <=69, "60-69","70-80"))



data2$AGE_GROUP <- ifelse(data2$AGE <40, "<40",
                          ifelse(data2$AGE >=40 &data2$AGE <=49, "40-49",
                                 ifelse(data2$AGE >=50 & data2$AGE <= 59, "50-59",
                                        ifelse(data2$AGE >=60 & data2$AGE <= 69,"60-69",
                                               ifelse(data2$AGE >=70 & data2$AGE <=80,"70-80",">80")))))

cci_list <- read_sas("D:/a/cci_list00389861.sas7bdat")
cci_list[,"cci_score"] = rowSums(cci_list[,c(2:14, 16:18)])
table(cci_list$cci_score)
nrow(cci_list)

data1 <- left_join(data1, cci_list, by="SN_KEY")
table(data1$cci_score)
sum(is.na(data1$cci_score)) #210개의 cci_score 결측값 발견#
data1$cci_score <- ifelse(is.na(data1$cci_score),0,data1$cci_score)
table(data1$cci_score)
nrow(cci_list)
nrow(data1)


data1$cci_group <- ifelse(data1$cci_score==0, "0",
                          ifelse(data1$cci_score <3, "1-2",
                                 ifelse(data1$cci_score <5, "3-4",
                                        ">=5")))
data1$cci_group <- ifelse(data1$cci_score==0, "0",
                          ifelse(data1$cci_score >=1 & data1$cci_score <=2, "1-2",
                                 ifelse(data1$cci_score >=3 & data1$cci_score <=4, "3-4",
                                        ">=5")))
table(data1$cci_group)

data11$smk_20 <- ifelse(is.na(data11$q_smk_pre_drt2) & is.na(data11$q_smk_now_drt2),"<20",
                       ifelse((!is.na(data11$q_smk_pre_drt2) & data11$q_smk_pre_drt2 >=20) | (!is.na(data11$q_smk_now_drt2) & data11$q_smk_now_drt2 >=20),">=20",
                              ifelse((!is.na(data11$q_smk_pre_drt2) & data11$q_smk_pre_drt2 <20) | (!is.na(data11$q_smk_now_drt2) & data11$q_smk_now_drt2 <20),"<20",NA)))

table(data11$smk_20)
sum(is.na(data11$smk_20))

View(data11[(data11$q_smk_pre_drt2 >=20 | data11$q_smk_now_drt2 >=20) ,c("q_smk_pre_drt2","q_smk_now_drt2")])

#all cause mortality#

person_years1 #225137.3#
number_of_events <- sum(data1$death_yn=="1")
number_of_events #49196#
print((number_of_events/person_years)*1000) #218.6709# 
print((number_of_events/person_years1)*1000) #218.5155#


round_half_up <- function(x,digits=0) {
  pong <-sign(x)
  z <- abs(x) * 10^digits
  z <- floor(z + 0.5)
  z <- z / 10^digits
  z * pong
}
data1$person_yearnew1 <- round_half_up(data1$day_diff/365,1)
sum(data1$person_yearnew1)


table(data1$grouptype)

png(filename = "survplot(second)1.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit1,legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred","darkgreen"),break.time.by=365, censor= FALSE, risk.table=TRUE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=TRUE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, ≥20PY smoking history","Aged 50-80, <20PY smoking history",
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        "Aged 50-80, never smoked"))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) + theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
p2<- p$table + theme(axis.text.y=element_text(size=15),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),plot.title=element_text(size=20),axis.text.x=element_blank(),axis.ticks.x=element_blank(),axis.title.x=element_blank(),panel.grid.major=element_blank(),panel.grid.minor=element_blank(),axis.line.x=element_blank(),axis.line.y=element_blank(),axis.ticks.y=element_blank())

g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g2 <- ggplotGrob(p2)

g2$widths <- g1$widths

g <- arrangeGrob(g1,g2,ncol=1, heights=c(3,1))

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

#cox hazard proportional model#
data1$grouptype <- factor(data1$grouptype)
data1$grouptype <- relevel(data1$grouptype,ref="001")
fit1 <- coxph(surv_all~grouptype,data=data1)
summary(fit1)
summary(fit1)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit1))

ifelse(round_half_up(summary(fit7)$coefficients[,5],3)<0.001,"<0.001",round_half_up(summary(fit7)$coefficients[,5],3))

#multivariable cox proportional hazard model#
data1$grouptype <- relevel(data1$grouptype,ref="001")
data1$AGE <- as.numeric(data1$AGE)
data1$SEX <- relevel(data1$SEX,ref="2")
data1$q_smk_yn2 <- relevel(data1$q_smk_yn2,ref="Never-smoker")
data1$pack_year <- as.numeric(data1$pack_year)
data1$G1E_BMI <- as.numeric(data1$G1E_BMI)
data1$income <- relevel(data1$income,ref="1")
data1$cci_group <- relevel(data1$cci_group,ref="0")
data1$activity <- relevel(data1$activity, ref="inactive")
data1$non_small_yn <- relevel(data1$non_small_yn,ref="0")
data1$SEER_GRP <- relevel(data1$SEER_GRP,ref="1")
data1$surgery_yn <- relevel(data1$surgery_yn,ref="no")
data1$radio_yn <- relevel(data1$radio_yn, ref="no")
data1$system_yn <- relevel(data1$system_yn, ref="no")
library(grid)
library(gridExtra)
data1$cci_group <-factor(data1$cci_group,levels=c("0","1-2","3-4",">=5"))


fit15 <- coxph(surv_all ~ grouptype+AGE+SEX+G1E_BMI+income+cci_group+activity
               +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn
                              ,data=as.data.frame(data1))

summary(fit15)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit15))
hr<-round_half_up(summary(fit15)$coefficients[,2],2)
lower <- round_half_up(exp(confint(fit15)),2)[,1]
upper <- round_half_up(exp(confint(fit15)),2)[,2]
paste0(hr,"(",lower,"-",upper,")")
ifelse(round_half_up(summary(fit15)$coefficients[,5],3)<0.001,"<0.001",round_half_up(summary(fit15)$coefficients[,5],3))


vif(fit15)
X <- model.matrix(~grouptype+AGE+SEX+q_smk_yn2+G1E_BMI+cci_group+activity
                  +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn,data=data1new)[,-1]
vif_values <- diag(solve(cor(X)))
vif_values

vif_manual12 <- sapply(1:ncol(X),
                       function(i) {
                         y <- X[,i]
                         X_others <- X[,-i]
                         R2 <- summary(lm(y ~ ., data= as.data.frame(X_others)))$r.squared
                         1/(1-R2)
                       })

names(vif_manual12) <- colnames(X)
vif_manual12


lm_fit1 <- lm(non_small_yn ~ grouptype,data=data1)






newdata <- data.frame(grouptype = c("001","002","003"), AGE=c(mean(data1$AGE),mean(data1$AGE),mean(data1$AGE)), SEX = c("2","2","2"),  
                      G1E_BMI = c(mean(data1$G1E_BMI),mean(data1$G1E_BMI),mean(data1$G1E_BMI)),income=c("1","1","1"),cci_group=c("0","0","0"),activity = c("inactive","inactive","inactive"),non_small_yn=c("0","0","0"), SEER_GRP=c("1","1","1"),surgery_yn=c("no","no","no"),radio_yn=c("no","no","no"),system_yn=c("no","no","no"))

newdata


surv_fit15 <- survfit(fit15,newdata=newdata,data=data1)

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          "Aged 50-80, never smoked"))

#lung cancer mortality에 대한 person_years, events#



#Lung cancer mortality#
data1$person_yearnew <- round(data1$day_diff/365,1)
person_years <- sum(data1$person_yearnew)
person_years #224977.4
person_years1 <- round(sum(data1$day_diff)/365,1)
person_years1 #225137.3#
lung_cancer_group <- data1[data1$lung_cancer_yn=="1",]
nrow(lung_cancer_group)#45271
number_of_events <- sum(lung_cancer_group$death_yn=="1")
print((number_of_events/person_years)*1000) #201.2247# 
print((number_of_events/person_years1)*1000) #201.0817#

#lung cancer mortality multivariable cox proportional hazard model#
data1$grouptype <- relevel(data1$grouptype,ref="001")
data1$AGE <- as.numeric(data1$AGE)
data1$SEX <- relevel(data1$SEX,ref="2")
data1$q_smk_yn2 <- relevel(data1$q_smk_yn2,ref="Never-smoker")
data1$pack_year <- as.numeric(data1$pack_year)
data1$G1E_BMI <- as.numeric(data1$G1E_BMI)
data1$income <- relevel(data1$income,ref="1")
data1$cci_group <- relevel(data1$cci_group,ref="0")
data1$activity <- relevel(data1$activity, ref="inactive")
data1$non_small_yn <- relevel(data1$non_small_yn,ref="0")
data1$SEER_GRP <- relevel(data1$SEER_GRP,ref="1")
data1$surgery_yn <- relevel(data1$surgery_yn,ref="no")
data1$radio_yn <- relevel(data1$radio_yn, ref="no")
data1$system_yn <- relevel(data1$system_yn, ref="no")



fit15 <- coxph(surv_lungcancer ~ grouptype+AGE+SEX+G1E_BMI+income+cci_group+activity
               +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn
               ,data=data1)

summary(fit15)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit15))
hr<-round_half_up(summary(fit15)$coefficients[,2],2)
lower <- round_half_up(exp(confint(fit15)),2)[,1]
upper <- round_half_up(exp(confint(fit15)),2)[,2]
paste0(hr,"(",lower,"-",upper,")")
ifelse(round_half_up(summary(fit15)$coefficients[,5],3)<0.001,"<0.001",round_half_up(summary(fit15)$coefficients[,5],3))

newdata <- data.frame(grouptype = c("001","002","003"), AGE=c(mean(data1$AGE),mean(data1$AGE),mean(data1$AGE)), SEX = c("2","2","2"), 
                      G1E_BMI = c(mean(data1$G1E_BMI),mean(data1$G1E_BMI),mean(data1$G1E_BMI)),income=c("1","1","1"),cci_group=c("0","0","0"),activity = c("inactive","inactive","inactive"),non_small_yn=c("0","0","0"), SEER_GRP=c("1","1","1"),surgery_yn=c("no","no","no"),radio_yn=c("no","no","no"),system_yn=c("no","no","no"))

newdata


surv_fit15 <- survfit(fit15,newdata=newdata,data=data1)
#cumulative hazard plot survfit으로 시도해보기#
png(filename = "cumhaznew03.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit15,fun="cumhaz",legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred","darkgreen"),break.time.by=365, censor= FALSE, risk.table=FALSE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=FALSE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, ≥20PY smoking history","Aged 50-80, <20PY smoking history",
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          "Aged 50-80, never smoked"))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) + theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g <- arrangeGrob(g1)

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

#두 번째 시각화 그래프#
ggsave(filename="cumhaznew03-1.png",width=18,height=10,plot=g,dpi=300)

              +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn,data=data11)

              +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn
              ,data=data11)

exp(confint(fit3))

gc()
newdata <- data.frame(smk_20 = c(">=20","<20"), AGE=c(mean(data11$AGE),mean(data11$AGE)), SEX = c("2","2"), q_smk_yn2 = c("Ex-smoker","Ex-smoker"), pack_year=c(mean(data11$pack_year),mean(data11$pack_year)),
                      G1E_BMI = c(mean(data11$G1E_BMI),mean(data11$G1E_BMI)),income=c("1","1"),cci_group=c("0","0"),activity = c("inactive","inactive"),non_small_yn=c("0","0"), SEER_GRP=c("1","1"),surgery_yn=c("no","no"),radio_yn=c("no","no"),system_yn=c("no","no"))

newdata


surv_fit3 <- survfit(fit3,newdata=newdata,data=data11)
#cumulative hazard plot survfit으로 시도해보기#
png(filename = "cumhaznew06.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit3,fun="cumhaz",legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred"),break.time.by=365, censor= FALSE, risk.table=FALSE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=FALSE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, ≥20 years smoking history","Aged 50-80, <20 years smoking history"
))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) + theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g <- arrangeGrob(g1)

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

#다섯번째 시각화 그래프#


surv_all <- Surv(data11$day_diff,data11$death_yn==1)
data11$lung_cancer_yn <- ifelse(is.na(data11$sain1new),0,ifelse(data11$sain1new==1,1,0))

surv_lungcancer <- Surv(data11$day_diff,data11$lung_cancer_yn =="1")
data11$smk_20 <- relevel(data11$smk_20,ref=">=20")

surv_fit4 <- survfit(surv_lungcancer ~smk_20, data=data11)
png(filename = "survplot(second)3.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit3,legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred"),break.time.by=365, censor= FALSE, risk.table=TRUE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=TRUE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, ≥20 years smoking history","Aged 50-80, <20 years smoking history"))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) +theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
p2<- p$table + theme(axis.text.y=element_text(size=15),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),plot.title=element_text(size=20),axis.text.x=element_blank(),axis.ticks.x=element_blank(),axis.title.x=element_blank(),panel.grid.major=element_blank(),panel.grid.minor=element_blank(),axis.line.x=element_blank(),axis.line.y=element_blank(),axis.ticks.y=element_blank())

g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g2 <- ggplotGrob(p2)

g2$widths <- g1$widths

g <- arrangeGrob(g1,g2,ncol=1, heights=c(3,1))

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

#All cause mortality에 대한 univariable cox proportional hazard analysis#

fit1 <- coxph(surv_all~grouptype,data=data3)
summary(fit1)
summary(fit1)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit1))

#multivariable cox proportional hazard model#
data3$grouptype <- relevel(data3$grouptype,ref="001")
data3$AGE <- as.numeric(data3$AGE)
data3$SEX <- relevel(data3$SEX,ref="2")
data3$q_smk_yn2 <- relevel(data3$q_smk_yn2,ref="Ex-smoker")
data3$pack_year <- as.numeric(data3$pack_year)
data3$G1E_BMI <- as.numeric(data3$G1E_BMI)
data3$income <- relevel(data3$income, ref="1")
data3$cci_group <- relevel(data3$cci_group,ref="0")
data3$activity <- relevel(data3$activity, ref="inactive")
data3$non_small_yn <- relevel(data3$non_small_yn,ref="0")
data3$SEER_GRP <- relevel(data3$SEER_GRP,ref="1")
data3$surgery_yn <- relevel(data3$surgery_yn,ref="no")
data3$radio_yn <- relevel(data3$radio_yn, ref="no")
data3$system_yn <- relevel(data3$system_yn, ref="no")


fit15 <- coxph(surv_all ~ grouptype+SEX+q_smk_yn2+pack_year+G1E_BMI+income+cci_group+activity
               +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn
               ,data=as.data.frame(data3))

summary(fit15)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit15))
vif(fit15)
X <- model.matrix(~grouptype+AGE+SEX+q_smk_yn2+pack_year+G1E_BMI+cci_group+activity
                  +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn,data=data3)[,-1]
vif_values <- diag(solve(cor(X)))
vif_values

vif_manual12 <- sapply(1:ncol(X),
              function(i) {
                y <- X[,i]
                X_others <- X[,-i]
                R2 <- summary(lm(y ~ ., data= as.data.frame(X_others)))$r.squared
                1/(1-R2)
              })

names(vif_manual12) <- colnames(X)
vif_manual12


lm_fit1 <- lm(non_small_yn ~ grouptype,data=data3)


fit15 <- coxph(surv_all ~ grouptype+system_yn+AGE
               ,data=as.data.frame(data3))

summary(fit15)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit15))

vif(lm_model)

C <- cor(X)

person_years # 77937.8#
person_years1 <- round(sum(data3[data3$grouptype=="001",]$day_diff)/365,1)
person_years1#77984.7#
number_of_events <- sum(lung_cancer_group[lung_cancer_group$grouptype=="001",]$death_yn=="1")
number_of_events # 21290
print((number_of_events/person_years)*1000) # 273.1666#
print((number_of_events/person_years1)*1000) # 273.0023#



#lung-cancer mortality group002#

person_years # 2577.4#

#lung_cancer mortality univariable cox 회귀분석#

data3$lung_cancer_yn <- ifelse(is.na(data3$sain1new),0,ifelse(data3$sain1new==1,1,0))
table(data3$lung_cancer_yn)

#cox hazard proportional model#
data3$grouptype <- relevel(data3$grouptype,ref="001")
fit1 <- coxph(surv_lungcancer~grouptype,data=data3)
summary(fit1)
summary(fit1)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit1))

#lung cancer mortality multivariable cox proportional hazard model#
data3$grouptype <- relevel(data3$grouptype,ref="001")
data3$AGE <- as.numeric(data3$AGE)
data3$SEX <- relevel(data3$SEX,ref="2")
data3$q_smk_yn2 <- relevel(data3$q_smk_yn2,ref="Ex-smoker")
data3$pack_year <- as.numeric(data3$pack_year)
data3$G1E_BMI <- as.numeric(data3$G1E_BMI)
data3$income <- relevel(data3$income,ref="1")
data3$cci_group <- relevel(data3$cci_group,ref="0")
data3$activity <- relevel(data3$activity, ref="inactive")
data3$non_small_yn <- relevel(data3$non_small_yn,ref="0")
data3$SEER_GRP <- relevel(data3$SEER_GRP,ref="1")
data3$surgery_yn <- relevel(data3$surgery_yn,ref="no")
data3$radio_yn <- relevel(data3$radio_yn, ref="no")
data3$system_yn <- relevel(data3$system_yn, ref="no")



fit15 <- coxph(surv_lungcancer ~ grouptype+SEX+q_smk_yn2+pack_year+G1E_BMI+income+cci_group+activity
               +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn
               ,data=as.data.frame(data3))

summary(fit15)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit15))
gc()

newdata <- data.frame(grouptype= c("001","002"),SEX=c("2","2"), q_smk_yn2 = c("Ex-smoker","Ex-smoker"), pack_year=c(mean(data3$pack_year),mean(data3$pack_year)),
                      G1E_BMI = c(mean(data3$G1E_BMI),mean(data3$G1E_BMI)),income=c("1","1"),cci_group=c("0","0"),activity = c("inactive","inactive"),non_small_yn=c("0","0"), SEER_GRP=c("1","1"),surgery_yn=c("no","no"),radio_yn=c("no","no"),system_yn=c("no","no"))

newdata


surv_fit15 <- survfit(fit15,newdata=newdata,data=data3)

#cumulative hazard plot survfit으로 시도해보기#
png(filename = "cumhaznew08.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit15,fun="cumhaz",legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred"),break.time.by=365, censor= FALSE, risk.table=FALSE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=FALSE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, ≥20PY smoking history","Aged 40-49, ≥20PY smoking history"
))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) + theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g <- arrangeGrob(g1)

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

#두 번째 시각화 그래프#
ggsave(filename="cumhaznew08-1.png",width=18,height=10,plot=g,dpi=300)

#새로운 군간 분석(1.50-80세 <20PY 흡연력 vs 40-49세 <20py ) #

nrow(data2)
sum(is.na(data2$pack_year))
data2$pack_year <- ifelse(is.na(data2$pack_year),0,data2$pack_year)
sum(is.na(data2$pack_year))
table(data2$pack_year)

                          ifelse(data2$AGE >=40 &data2$AGE <=49, "40-49",
                                 ifelse(data2$AGE >=50 & data2$AGE <= 59, "50-59",
                                        ifelse(data2$AGE >=60 & data2$AGE <= 69,"60-69",
                                               ifelse(data2$AGE >=70 & data2$AGE <=80,"70-80",">80")))))

#keplan-meier#

library(grid)
data4$grouptype <- as.factor(data4$grouptype)
table(data4$grouptype)

getwd()

#첫번째 시각화 그래프#
png(filename = "survplot(second)7.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit1,legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred"),break.time.by=365,censor= FALSE, risk.table=TRUE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=TRUE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, <20PY smoking history","Aged 40-49, <20PY smoking history"))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) + theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
p2<- p$table + theme(axis.text.y=element_text(size=15),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),plot.title=element_text(size=20),axis.text.x=element_blank(),axis.ticks.x=element_blank(),axis.title.x=element_blank(),panel.grid.major=element_blank(),panel.grid.minor=element_blank(),axis.line.x=element_blank(),axis.line.y=element_blank(),axis.ticks.y=element_blank())

g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g2 <- ggplotGrob(p2)

g2$widths <- g1$widths

g <- arrangeGrob(g1,g2,ncol=1, heights=c(3,1))

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

#All cause mortality에 대한 univariable cox proportional hazard analysis#
#cox hazard proportional model#
data4$grouptype <- factor(data4$grouptype)
data4$grouptype <- relevel(data4$grouptype,ref="001")
fit1 <- coxph(surv_all~grouptype,data=data4)
summary(fit1)
summary(fit1)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit1))


data4$grouptype <- relevel(data4$grouptype,ref="002")

#multivariable cox proportional hazard model#
data4$grouptype <- relevel(data4$grouptype,ref="001")
data4$AGE <- as.numeric(data4$AGE)
data4$SEX <- relevel(data4$SEX,ref="2")
data4$q_smk_yn2 <- relevel(data4$q_smk_yn2,ref="Ex-smoker")
data4$pack_year <- as.numeric(data4$pack_year)
data4$G1E_BMI <- as.numeric(data4$G1E_BMI)
data4$income <- relevel(data4$income,ref="1")
data4$cci_group <- relevel(data4$cci_group,ref="0")
data4$activity <- relevel(data4$activity, ref="inactive")
data4$non_small_yn <- relevel(data4$non_small_yn,ref="0")
data4$SEER_GRP <- relevel(data4$SEER_GRP,ref="1")
data4$surgery_yn <- relevel(data4$surgery_yn,ref="no")
data4$radio_yn <- relevel(data4$radio_yn, ref="no")
data4$system_yn <- relevel(data4$system_yn, ref="no")


fit15 <- coxph(surv_all ~ grouptype+SEX+q_smk_yn2+pack_year+G1E_BMI+income+cci_group+activity
               +non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn
               ,data=as.data.frame(data4))

summary(fit15)$coefficients
exp(confint(fit15))
gc()
newdata <- data.frame(grouptype= c("001","002"), SEX = c("2","2"), q_smk_yn2 = c("Ex-smoker","Ex-smoker"), pack_year=c(mean(data4$pack_year),mean(data4$pack_year)),
                      G1E_BMI = c(mean(data4$G1E_BMI),mean(data4$G1E_BMI)),income=c("1","1"),cci_group=c("0","0"),activity = c("inactive","inactive"),non_small_yn=c("0","0"), SEER_GRP=c("1","1"),surgery_yn=c("no","no"),radio_yn=c("no","no"),system_yn=c("no","no"))

newdata


surv_fit15 <- survfit(fit15,newdata=newdata,data=data4)

#cumulative hazard plot survfit으로 시도해보기#
png(filename = "cumhaznew09.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit15,fun="cumhaz",legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred"),break.time.by=365, censor= FALSE, risk.table=FALSE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=FALSE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, <20PY smoking history","Aged 40-49, <20PY smoking history"
))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) + theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g <- arrangeGrob(g1)

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

exp(confint(fit15))

                      G1E_BMI = c(mean(data4$G1E_BMI),mean(data4$G1E_BMI)),income=c("1","1"),cci_group=c("0","0"),activity = c("inactive","inactive"),non_small_yn=c("0","0"), SEER_GRP=c("1","1"),surgery_yn=c("no","no"),radio_yn=c("no","no"),system_yn=c("no","no"))

newdata


surv_fit15 <- survfit(fit15,newdata=newdata,data=data4)

#cumulative hazard plot survfit으로 시도해보기#
png(filename = "cumhaznew010.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit15,fun="cumhaz",legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred"),break.time.by=365, censor= FALSE, risk.table=FALSE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=FALSE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, <20PY smoking history","Aged 40-49, <20PY smoking history"
))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) + theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g <- arrangeGrob(g1)

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

#두 번째 시각화 그래프#
ggsave(filename="cumhaznew010-1.png",width=18,height=10,plot=g,dpi=300)

#all cause mortality log rank test#


library(survival)
surv_all <- Surv(data5$day_diff,data5$death_yn==1)
data5$lung_cancer_yn <- ifelse(is.na(data5$sain1new),0,ifelse(data5$sain1new==1,1,0))
table(data5$lung_cancer_yn)
surv_lungcancer <- Surv(data5$day_diff,data5$lung_cancer_yn ==1)


#all cause mortality#
data5$person_yearnew <- round(data5$day_diff/365,1)
person_years <- sum(data5$person_yearnew)
person_years #121455.4
person_years1 <- round(sum(data5$day_diff)/365,1)
person_years1 #121548.4#
number_of_events <- sum(data5$death_yn=="1")
number_of_events #18281#
print((number_of_events/person_years)*1000) #150.5162# 
print((number_of_events/person_years1)*1000) #150.401#



data5$person_yearnew1 <- round_half_up(data5$day_diff/365,1)
person_years <- sum(data5$person_yearnew1)
person_years #121455.4

#all-cause mortality group001#

person_years <- sum(data5[data5$grouptype=="001",]$person_yearnew)
person_years #112926.4#
person_years1 <- round(sum(data5[data5$grouptype=="001",]$day_diff)/365,1)
person_years1 #113012.8#
number_of_events <- sum(data5[data5$grouptype=="001",]$death_yn=="1")
number_of_events # 17570
print((number_of_events/person_years)*1000) # 155.5881#
print((number_of_events/person_years1)*1000) #155.4691#


person_years <- sum(data5[data5$grouptype=="001",]$person_yearnew1)
person_years #112926.4#

#All cause mortality에 대한 univariable cox proportional hazard analysis#
#cox hazard proportional model#
data5$grouptype <- factor(data5$grouptype)

#cumulative hazard plot survfit으로 시도해보기#

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit15,fun="cumhaz",legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred"),break.time.by=365, censor= FALSE, risk.table=FALSE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=FALSE, xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Aged 50-80, never smoked","Aged 40-49, never smoked"
))

person_years #121455.4#

person_years1 #121548.4#

nrow(lung_cancer_group)#16776
number_of_events <- sum(lung_cancer_group$death_yn=="1")
number_of_events

print((number_of_events/person_years1)*1000) #138.0191#

number_of_events # 16114

#keplan-meier#

library(grid)
data6$grouptype <- as.factor(data6$grouptype)
table(data6$grouptype)

getwd()
data6$grouptype <- relevel(data6$grouptype,ref="001")
surv_fit1 <- survfit(surv_all ~grouptype, data=data6)
surv_fit2 <- survfit(surv_lungcancer ~grouptype, data=data6)


#첫번째 시각화 그래프#
png(filename = "survplot(second)11.png",width=2200,height= 1400,res=100)

p<- ggsurvplot(surv_fit1,legend.title="",palette = c("darkblue","darkred"),break.time.by=365,censor= FALSE, risk.table=TRUE, risk.table.y.text.col= FALSE, conf.int=TRUE,xlab= "Time since diagnosis (years)",legend.labs=c("Included in the analysis","Excluded from the analysis"))
p1 <- p$plot + scale_x_continuous(labels= c(0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8),breaks=c(0,365,730,1095,1460,1825,2190,2555,2920)) + theme(axis.title.x=element_text(size=20),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),axis.title.y=element_text(size=20,hjust=0.5),legend.text=element_text(size=15),axis.text.x=element_text(size=20),axis.text.y=element_text(size=20))
p2<- p$table + theme(axis.text.y=element_text(size=15),plot.margin=margin(5,70,5,290),plot.title=element_text(size=20),axis.text.x=element_blank(),axis.ticks.x=element_blank(),axis.title.x=element_blank(),panel.grid.major=element_blank(),panel.grid.minor=element_blank(),axis.line.x=element_blank(),axis.line.y=element_blank(),axis.ticks.y=element_blank())

g1 <- ggplotGrob(p1)
g2 <- ggplotGrob(p2)

g2$widths <- g1$widths

g <- arrangeGrob(g1,g2,ncol=1, heights=c(3,1))

grid.newpage()
grid.draw(g)

dev.off()

#두 번째 시각화 그래프#
ggsave(filename="survplot(second)11-1.png",width=18,height=10,plot=g,dpi=300)

median_fun <- function(data, indices)
{
  median(data[indices], na.rm=TRUE)
}

sum(is.na(data1$dth_type))
gc()

data1$non_small_yn <- relevel(data1$non_small_yn, ref="1")
library(cmprsk)
data1$dth_type <- NULL
m1<-model.matrix(~grouptype+AGE+SEX+G1E_BMI+income
                 +cci_group+activity+non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn,data=data1)[,-1]
crr1_1_3 <- crr(ftime=data1$day_diff,fstatus=data1$dth_type, cencode=0, cov1=m1, failcode=1)
summary(crr1_1_3)
exp(crr1_1_3$coef)
beta<- crr1_1_3$coef
se <- sqrt(diag(crr1_1_3$var))
hr <- exp(beta)
lower <- exp(beta - 1.96*se)
upper <- exp(beta + 1.96*se)
result <- data.frame(HR = hr, lower95=lower, upper95=upper)
result
s<- summary(crr1_1_3)
zval <- s$coef[,"z"]
pval <- 2*(1-pnorm(abs(zval)))
cbind(s$coef,pval)

predict(crr1_1_3,cov1=m1[1,],cov2=m1[2,])

fgr1_1_1 <-FGR()

data11$grouptype <- relevel(data11$smk_20,ref="<20")
data11$AGE <- as.numeric(data11$AGE)
data11$SEX <- relevel(data11$SEX,ref="2")
data11$q_smk_yn2 <- relevel(data11$q_smk_yn2,ref="Ex-smoker")
data11$pack_year <- as.numeric(data11$pack_year)
data11$G1E_BMI <- as.numeric(data11$G1E_BMI)
data11$income <- relevel(data11$income,ref="1")
data11$cci_group <- relevel(data11$cci_group,ref="0")
data11$activity <- relevel(data11$activity, ref="inactive")
data11$non_small_yn <- relevel(data11$non_small_yn,ref="1")
data11$SEER_GRP <- relevel(data11$SEER_GRP,ref="1")
data11$surgery_yn <- relevel(data11$surgery_yn,ref="no")
data11$radio_yn <- relevel(data11$radio_yn, ref="no")
data11$system_yn <- relevel(data11$system_yn, ref="no")

#data111#
data11[data11$death_yn==0,"dth_type"] = 0
data11[data11$lung_cancer_yn==1,"dth_type"] = 1
data11[data11$sain1new ==2 & !is.na(data11$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 2
data11[data11$sain1new ==3 & !is.na(data11$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 3
data11[data11$sain1new ==5 & !is.na(data11$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 4
data11[data11$sain1new ==6 & !is.na(data11$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 5
data11[data11$sain1new ==7 & !is.na(data11$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 6
data11[data11$sain1new ==9 & !is.na(data11$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 7
data11[data11$sain1new =="Other" & !is.na(data11$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 8
table(data11$dth_type)
data11[is.na(data11$dth_type ==TRUE),"dth_type"] = 2

data3$grouptype <- relevel(data3$grouptype, ref="002")
data3$non_small_yn <- relevel(data3$non_small_yn,ref="1")
m1<-model.matrix(~grouptype+SEX+q_smk_yn2+pack_year+G1E_BMI
                 +cci_group+activity+non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn,data=data3)[,-1] 
View(m1)
crr3_2 <- crr(ftime=data3$day_diff,fstatus=data3$dth_type, cencode=0, cov1=m1, failcode=1)
summary(crr3_2)
exp(crr3_2$coef)
beta<- crr3_2$coef
se <- sqrt(diag(crr3_2$var))
hr <- exp(beta)
lower <- exp(beta - 1.96*se)
upper <- exp(beta + 1.96*se)
result <- data.frame(HR = hr, lower95=lower, upper95=upper)
result
s<- summary(crr3_2)
zval <- s$coef[,"z"]
pval <- 2*(1-pnorm(abs(zval)))
cbind(s$coef,pval)

#data4를 활용하여 gray fine model 정리#
data4$grouptype <- relevel(data4$grouptype,ref="002")
data4$AGE <- as.numeric(data4$AGE)
data4$SEX <- relevel(data4$SEX,ref="2")
data4$q_smk_yn2 <- relevel(data4$q_smk_yn2,ref="Ex-smoker")
data4$pack_year <- as.numeric(data4$pack_year)
data4$G1E_BMI <- as.numeric(data4$G1E_BMI)
data4$income <- relevel(data4$income,ref="1")
data4$cci_group <- relevel(data4$cci_group,ref="0")
data4$activity <- relevel(data4$activity, ref="inactive")
data4$non_small_yn <- relevel(data4$non_small_yn,ref="1")
data4$SEER_GRP <- relevel(data4$SEER_GRP,ref="1")
data4$surgery_yn <- relevel(data4$surgery_yn,ref="no")
data4$radio_yn <- relevel(data4$radio_yn, ref="no")
data4$system_yn <- relevel(data4$system_yn, ref="no")



data4[data4$death_yn==0,"dth_type"] = 0
data4[data4$lung_cancer_yn==1,"dth_type"] = 1
data4[data4$sain1new ==2 & !is.na(data4$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 2
data4[data4$sain1new ==3 & !is.na(data4$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 3
data4[data4$sain1new ==5 & !is.na(data4$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 4
data4[data4$sain1new ==6 & !is.na(data4$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 5
data4[data4$sain1new ==7 & !is.na(data4$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 6
data4[data4$sain1new ==9 & !is.na(data4$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 7
data4[data4$sain1new =="Other" & !is.na(data4$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 8
table(data4$dth_type)
data4[is.na(data4$dth_type ==TRUE),"dth_type"] = 2
sum(is.na(data4$dth_type))


View(m1)
library(cmprsk)


table(data3$smk_20)

                 +cci_group+activity+non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn,data=data4)[,-1]

beta<- crr4_12$coef
se <- sqrt(diag(crr4_12$var))
hr <- exp(beta)
lower <- exp(beta - 1.96*se)
upper <- exp(beta + 1.96*se)
result <- data.frame(HR = hr, lower95=lower, upper95=upper)
result
s<- summary(crr4_12)
zval <- s$coef[,"z"]
pval <- 2*(1-pnorm(abs(zval)))
cbind(s$coef,pval)

#data5에 대한 grayfine model 정리#
data5$grouptype <- relevel(data5$grouptype,ref="002")
data5$AGE <- as.numeric(data5$AGE)
data5$SEX <- relevel(data5$SEX,ref="2")
data5$q_smk_yn2 <- relevel(data5$q_smk_yn2,ref="Ex-smoker")
data5$pack_year <- as.numeric(data5$pack_year)
data5$G1E_BMI <- as.numeric(data5$G1E_BMI)
data5$cci_group <- relevel(data5$cci_group,ref="0")
data5$activity <- relevel(data5$activity, ref="inactive")
data5$non_small_yn <- relevel(data5$non_small_yn,ref="1")
data5$SEER_GRP <- relevel(data5$SEER_GRP,ref="1")
data5$surgery_yn <- relevel(data5$surgery_yn,ref="no")
data5$radio_yn <- relevel(data5$radio_yn, ref="no")
data5$system_yn <- relevel(data5$system_yn, ref="no")

data5[data5$death_yn==0,"dth_type"] = 0
data5[data5$lung_cancer_yn==1,"dth_type"] = 1
data5[data5$sain1new ==2 & !is.na(data5$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 2
data5[data5$sain1new ==3 & !is.na(data5$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 3
data5[data5$sain1new ==5 & !is.na(data5$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 4
data5[data5$sain1new ==6 & !is.na(data5$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 5
data5[data5$sain1new ==7 & !is.na(data5$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 6
data5[data5$sain1new ==9 & !is.na(data5$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 7
data5[data5$sain1new =="Other" & !is.na(data5$sain1new),"dth_type"] = 8
table(data5$dth_type)
data5[is.na(data5$dth_type ==TRUE),"dth_type"] = 2
sum(is.na(data5$dth_type))


m1<-model.matrix(~grouptype+SEX+G1E_BMI+income
                 +cci_group+activity+non_small_yn+SEER_GRP+surgery_yn+radio_yn+system_yn,data=data5)[,-1]

result
